DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player pilots an ever‑accelerating dot, tapping to instantly flip its direction and weave through waves of spikes that spawn ahead of its current travel path.

## Player
The player controls a simple glowing dot. Verbs: flip direction (tap), maintain momentum (continuous), dodge (reactive movement).

## Core loop
Each moment the dot surges forward while the player watches incoming spikes appear along the forward line. The player decides the precise instant to tap and reverse velocity, timing flips to slip between spikes and stay alive. Successive flips become rhythmically tighter, rewarding quick perception and split‑second decision‑making.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Wall spikes** – static spikes that materialize on the wall directly ahead of the dot’s motion; they remain until the dot passes or flips away.
- **Moving sweepers** – long bars that slide down the wall in the direction of travel, sweeping across the path and forcing the player to flip before contact.
- **Burst emitters** – nodes that fire short bursts of spikes outward in a cone aligned with the current travel direction, creating temporary clusters to navigate.

## Score
- Flipping successfully without hitting a spike.
- Surviving a full wave of spikes (a set of obstacles that appears before a pause).
- Executing a chain of consecutive flips (combo) without collision.

## Win and lose
The player loses the moment the dot collides with any spike; the screen flashes and the dot shatters. The player wins by surviving a predefined number of waves or reaching a distant “exit portal” that appears after the final wave, accompanied by a bright burst and celebratory visual.

## Feel
A fast‑paced, reflex‑driven experience with a neon‑glow aesthetic against a dark, minimalist backdrop; the soundscape is tight chiptune synths and crisp arcade‑style beeps that punctuate each flip and collision.

## Why it's fun
The thrill of snapping direction at the exact instant a wall spikes materializes, turning a split‑second twitch into a satisfying dodge that feels like mastering momentum.

TASKS.md

show
1. HTML Scaffold — Create index.html with header, description, controls list, canvas, restart button, stub script tag, and matching meta.json file.
2. Game State Objects — Define player, hazards, score, and phase objects with initial values at top of script.
3. Animation Loop — Implement startLoop() using requestAnimationFrame, with cancelAnimationFrame handling, try/catch, and clear canvas.
4. Input Handling — Set up keys Set with keydown/keyup listeners, R-key restart, and restart button click invoking reset().
5. Player Movement — Add dt‑based physics for acceleration, velocity reversal on tap, speed caps, and bounds clamping.
6. Hazard System — Create hazard data structure, spawn logic with SAFE_RADIUS, and dt‑scaled motion.
7. Collision Detection — Detect player‑hazard collisions, switch phase to "gameover", and halt gameplay.
8. Scoring — Increment score on events, render score text at top‑left of canvas.
9. Rendering — Draw background, hazards, player (high contrast), and HUD each frame.
10. Game‑Over Overlay — Dim screen, show "Game Over", final score, and restart instructions.
11. Observable Contract — Expose window.__game each frame with state, score, player visibility, and objective description.
12. Audio System — Initialize AudioContext lazily, add beep() helper, and hook sounds to hit, score, and death events.

// === CONFIG =============================================================
const W = 800, H = 600;
const SAFE_RADIUS = 200; // no hazard spawns within this radius of player at start
const HAZARD_SPEED = 150; // px/sec
const HAZARD_RADIUS = 6;

// Audio system (lazy init)
let audioCtx = null;
function getAudioCtx(){
  if (!audioCtx) {
    audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
  }
  return audioCtx;
}
function beep(freq, dur){
  const ctx = getAudioCtx();
  const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
  const gain = ctx.createGain();
  osc.frequency.value = freq;
  osc.connect(gain);
  gain.connect(ctx.destination);
  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.1, ctx.currentTime);
  osc.start();
  osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + dur);
}
    // === STATE =============================================================
    const player = { x: W/2, y: H/2, vx: 0, vy: 0 };
    const hazards = [];
    let score = 0;
    let phase = "menu"; // "menu" | "playing" | "gameover"
    const keys = new Set();
    const objective = "Flip momentum to avoid spikes coming from the direction you travel.";
    // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
function publishState() {
  window.__game = {
    state: phase,
    score,
    player: {
      x: player.x,
      y: player.y,
      visible: phase !== 'gameover' // hide player on game over
    },
    objective
  };
}
    // Initial publish before any rendering.
    publishState();

// Ensure canvas element is obtained early
const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

// === RENDER =============================================================
function render() {
  // background
  ctx.fillStyle = '#05050c';
  ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);

  // draw hazards (high contrast red)
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4d4d';
  for (const h of hazards) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(h.x, h.y, HAZARD_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
  }

  // draw player (high contrast cyan)
  ctx.fillStyle = '#7df9ff';
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, 4, 0, Math.PI*2);
  ctx.fill();

  // HUD: score and phase
  ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
  ctx.font = '16px sans-serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'top';
  ctx.fillText(`Score: ${score}`, 10, 10);
  ctx.fillText(`Phase: ${phase}`, 10, 30);

  // Game Over overlay
  if (phase === 'gameover') {
    // dim background
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    // text
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4d4d';
    ctx.font = '48px sans-serif';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    ctx.fillText('Game Over', W/2, H/2 - 30);
    ctx.font = '24px sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText(`Final Score: ${score}`, W/2, H/2 + 10);
    ctx.fillText('Press R or click Restart', W/2, H/2 + 50);
  }
}

// === UPDATE =============================================================
function update(dt) {
  // accelerate forward (increase speed magnitude)
  const speed = Math.hypot(player.vx, player.vy);
  const dir = speed === 0 ? {x: 1, y: 0} : {x: player.vx / speed, y: player.vy / speed};
  // increase speed
  let newSpeed = Math.min(speed + ACCEL * dt, MAX_SPEED);
  player.vx = dir.x * newSpeed;
  player.vy = dir.y * newSpeed;
  // move
  player.x += player.vx * dt;
  player.y += player.vy * dt;
  // clamp to bounds
  if (player.x < 0) player.x = 0;
  if (player.x > W) player.x = W;
  if (player.y < 0) player.y = 0;
  if (player.y > H) player.y = H;

  // === COLLISION DETECTION =================================================
  // Check collision with hazards; if any, end game
  for (const h of hazards) {
    const dx = player.x - h.x;
    const dy = player.y - h.y;
    const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
    const radSum = 4 + HAZARD_RADIUS; // player radius 4
    if (distSq < radSum * radSum) {
      phase = "gameover";
      // Stop the game loop
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      break;
    }
  }
}

    // === LOOP =============================================================
    let loopId = null;
    let lastTime = performance.now();
    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      phase = "playing";
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;
          // Update player movement
    update(dt);
    // placeholder other update logic could go here
          render();
          publishState();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('loop error', err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    // === INPUT =============================================================
    function reset() { location.reload(); }
    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'r' || e.key === 'R') {
        reset();
        return;
      }
      keys.add(e.key);
    });
    document.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => { keys.delete(e.key); });
    // Tap / click to flip velocity (placeholder, actual logic later)
canvas.addEventListener('click', () => {
    // Reverse velocity instantly
    player.vx = -player.vx;
    player.vy = -player.vy;
    beep(400, 0.05); // flip sound
  });
    document.getElementById('restart').addEventListener('click', reset);
    // === BOOT ===============================================================
    startLoop();

## Reference example: "asteroids"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Asteroids</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Rotate, thrust, and shoot. Big rocks split into small ones. Get hit and you're gone.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Left/Right (or A/D) to rotate</li>
      <li>Up (or W) to thrust</li>
      <li>Space to fire</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>

  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const W = 800, H = 600;
  const SHIP_RADIUS = 12;
  const SHIP_THRUST = 280;       // px/sec^2
  const SHIP_FRICTION = 0.6;     // velocity decay per second (1 = none)
  const SHIP_TURN = Math.PI * 1.8; // rad/sec
  const BULLET_SPEED = 520;      // px/sec
  const BULLET_LIFE = 1.0;       // sec
  const FIRE_COOLDOWN = 0.20;    // sec between shots
  const ASTEROID_BIG_R = 38;
  const ASTEROID_MID_R = 22;
  const ASTEROID_LIL_R = 12;
  const ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE = [40, 110];
  const SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN = 160; // no asteroid spawns inside this disk

  const SCORE_BIG = 20, SCORE_MID = 50, SCORE_LIL = 100;

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let ship;        // { x, y, vx, vy, angle, alive }
  let bullets;     // [{ x, y, vx, vy, life }]
  let asteroids;   // [{ x, y, vx, vy, r, shape: number[] }]
  let score;
  let highScore = 0;
  let phase;       // "playing" | "gameover"
  let fireCooldown;
  let keys;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    ship = { x: W / 2, y: H / 2, vx: 0, vy: 0, angle: -Math.PI / 2, alive: true };
    bullets = [];
    asteroids = [];
    spawnWave(5);
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    fireCooldown = 0;
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  function spawnWave(n) {
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) spawnAsteroid(ASTEROID_BIG_R);
  }

  function spawnAsteroid(r) {
    let x, y, tries = 0;
    do {
      x = Math.random() * W;
      y = Math.random() * H;
      tries++;
    } while (Math.hypot(x - ship.x, y - ship.y) < SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN && tries < 30);
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const speed = ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0] + Math.random() * (ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[1] - ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0]);
    asteroids.push({
      x, y,
      vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
      vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
      r,
      shape: makeAsteroidShape(),
    });
  }

  function makeAsteroidShape() {
    // Per-vertex radius multipliers, 8-12 vertices for a knobbly silhouette.
    const n = 8 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);
    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) out.push(0.75 + Math.random() * 0.45);
    return out;
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Ship.
    if (ship.alive) {
      const turnIn = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
                   - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
      ship.angle += turnIn * SHIP_TURN * dt;

      const thrusting = keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W");
      if (thrusting) {
        ship.vx += Math.cos(ship.angle) * SHIP_THRUST * dt;
        ship.vy += Math.sin(ship.angle) * SHIP_THRUST * dt;
      }
      // Mild friction so the ship doesn't drift forever.
      const decay = Math.pow(SHIP_FRICTION, dt);
      ship.vx *= decay;
      ship.vy *= decay;
      ship.x = wrap(ship.x + ship.vx * dt, W);
      ship.y = wrap(ship.y + ship.vy * dt, H);

      fireCooldown -= dt;
      if ((keys.has(" ") || keys.has("Space")) && fireCooldown <= 0) {
        fire();
        fireCooldown = FIRE_COOLDOWN;
      }
    }

    // Bullets.
    for (const b of bullets) {
      b.life -= dt;
      b.x = wrap(b.x + b.vx * dt, W);
      b.y = wrap(b.y + b.vy * dt, H);
    }
    bullets = bullets.filter((b) => b.life > 0);

    // Asteroids.
    for (const a of asteroids) {
      a.x = wrap(a.x + a.vx * dt, W);
      a.y = wrap(a.y + a.vy * dt, H);
    }

    // Bullet-asteroid collisions.
    for (let i = asteroids.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const a = asteroids[i];
      let hit = false;
      for (let j = bullets.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
        const b = bullets[j];
        if (Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y) < a.r) {
          bullets.splice(j, 1);
          hit = true;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (hit) splitAsteroid(i);
    }

    // Ship-asteroid collisions.
    if (ship.alive) {
      for (const a of asteroids) {
        if (Math.hypot(a.x - ship.x, a.y - ship.y) < a.r + SHIP_RADIUS * 0.6) {
          ship.alive = false;
          phase = "gameover";
          if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
          beep(110, 0.3);
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    // Wave clear -> spawn next.
    if (asteroids.length === 0 && phase === "playing") {
      spawnWave(5);
    }
  }

  function fire() {
    bullets.push({
      x: ship.x + Math.cos(ship.angle) * SHIP_RADIUS,
      y: ship.y + Math.sin(ship.angle) * SHIP_RADIUS,
      vx: ship.vx + Math.cos(ship.angle) * BULLET_SPEED,
      vy: ship.vy + Math.sin(ship.angle) * BULLET_SPEED,
      life: BULLET_LIFE,
    });
    beep(820, 0.04);
  }

  function splitAsteroid(idx) {
    const a = asteroids[idx];
    asteroids.splice(idx, 1);
    if (a.r === ASTEROID_BIG_R) {
      score += SCORE_BIG;
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) addChild(a, ASTEROID_MID_R);
      beep(440, 0.06);
    } else if (a.r === ASTEROID_MID_R) {
      score += SCORE_MID;
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) addChild(a, ASTEROID_LIL_R);
      beep(560, 0.05);
    } else {
      score += SCORE_LIL;
      beep(700, 0.05);
    }
  }

  function addChild(parent, r) {
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const speed = ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0] + Math.random() * (ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[1] - ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0]);
    asteroids.push({
      x: parent.x, y: parent.y,
      vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
      vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
      r,
      shape: makeAsteroidShape(),
    });
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    // Asteroids.
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#a5a5b8";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    for (const a of asteroids) drawAsteroid(a);

    // Bullets.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffd23f";
    for (const b of bullets) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, 2.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }

    // Ship.
    if (ship.alive) drawShip();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Best ${highScore}`, W - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "gameover") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(5,5,12,0.65)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText("Game Over", W / 2, H / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, W / 2, H / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", W / 2, H / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  function drawShip() {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(ship.x, ship.y);
    ctx.rotate(ship.angle);
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(SHIP_RADIUS, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.9, SHIP_RADIUS * 0.7);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.5, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.9, -SHIP_RADIUS * 0.7);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.stroke();
    if (keys && (keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W"))) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#ffd23f";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.5, 0);
      ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS - 6 - Math.random() * 4, 0);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function drawAsteroid(a) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i < a.shape.length; i++) {
      const t = (i / a.shape.length) * Math.PI * 2;
      const r = a.r * a.shape[i];
      const x = a.x + Math.cos(t) * r;
      const y = a.y + Math.sin(t) * r;
      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y); else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
    if (ev.key === " ") ev.preventDefault();
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "triangle";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase,                                  // "playing" | "gameover"
      score,
      player: {
        x: ship.x,
        y: ship.y,
        // wrap() keeps coords in [0, W) and [0, H), so visible is always true
        // — but assert it explicitly so the contract is honest.
        visible: ship.x >= 0 && ship.x < W && ship.y >= 0 && ship.y < H,
      },
      objective: "Shoot the asteroids before they hit your ship. Big rocks split into smaller ones.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function wrap(v, max) {
    if (v < 0) return v + max;
    if (v >= max) return v - max;
    return v;
  }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

Never make the player wait.\n92: 2. **Clear feedback** — the player always knows when something happened. Use color flashes, particles, brief screen shake, score popups, or sound on every meaningful event (hit, score, miss, death).\n93: 3. **Win/lose state** — the game has a clear end condition. On lose, draw \"Game Over\" + final score + restart instructions on the canvas. On win, celebrate (color burst, \"You Win!\" overlay).\n94: 4. **Restart without page reload** — both pressing R AND clicking the `<button id=\"restart\">` reset the game cleanly via the same code path. Mention both in `controls` (e.g. \"Press R or click Restart\"). On restart you MUST `cancelAnimationFrame` any pending loop before starting a new one (otherwise loops stack on each restart and the game eventually slows to a crawl).\n95: 5. **Progressive difficulty** — start gentle so the player can learn, then ramp up. Don't dump them into chaos at t=0.\n96: 6. **No dead time** — something interesting on screen at all times. Idle background animation (stars, gradient pulse, particles) is fine.\n97: 7. **Persistent score / progress UI** — drawn on the canvas at the top corner, always visible.\n98: 8. **Audio (optional but encouraged)** — generate tones with `OscillatorNode` (no external assets). **Do not auto-start audio on page load** — browsers block this and it will throw. Initialize the `AudioContext` lazily on the first keypress or click. Wrap audio code in try/catch.\n99: \n100: ## Playable Contract — non-negotiable\n101: \n102: These are not aspirations. A game that violates any of these is broken even if it runs without errors. After your implementation finishes, the validator loads the page in a real browser and runs an 8-second playtest with synthetic input — failures here are reported back to you and must be fixed before the game ships.\n103: \n104: ### 1. The player can lose\n105: \n106: There must be a collision check (or other end condition) that fires `gameOver` and stops the active gameplay loop. \"Game runs forever, no death\" is broken. Examples that have failed in the past: collision check compared a value against itself; \"spike\" collision only clamped position instead of killing the player; obstacles never reached the player.\n107: \n108: ### 2. The player can score\n109: \n110: The score must increase on a **discrete event** (a survived obstacle, a collected pickup, an enemy destroyed, a beat hit). \"Score = elapsed seconds\" is allowed but weak. Score must be drawn on the canvas at all times.\n111: \n112: ### 3. The first 3 seconds are survivable with no input\n113: \n114: Define a `SAFE_RADIUS` (e.g. 200px) around the player's starting position. When spawning hazards, **reject any spawn that is within `SAFE_RADIUS` of the player AND would reach them within 3 seconds at current hazard speed**. Pre-spawning hazards on top of the player at t=0 has caused frame-1 deaths in past games. Don't do it.\n115: \n116: ### 4. The control actually changes the outcome\n117: \n118: Whatever the player presses must, in the next frame, change a value that affects collision or score. If a control is purely cosmetic (e.g. \"stretch horizontally\" but the gaps are vertical), the game is fundamentally broken even if it runs. After writing, **re-read the game's concept and trace one frame**: does the input do what the concept claims?\n119: \n120: ### 5. dt-based physics, not per-frame constants\n121: \n122: All movement and timing must scale by delta time (seconds since last frame). Per-frame constants (`x += 5` every frame) make games run 2.4× faster on a 144Hz display.\n123: \n124: ```js\n125: let lastTime = performance.now();\n126: function loop(now) {\n127:   const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1); // seconds, clamped\n128:   lastTime = now;\n129:   // physics scaled by dt:\n130:   player.x += player.vx * dt;\n131:   enemy.y  += enemy.vy  * dt;\n132:   spawnTimer -= dt;\n133:   // ...\n134:   requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n135: }\n136: requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n137: ```\n138: \n139: ### 6. Restart fully resets AND cancels the prior loop\n140: \n141: Both R and the Restart button must call the same reset function. That function MUST `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` the previous loop before scheduling a new one — otherwise loops stack on each restart and the game slows to a crawl over time.\n142: \n143: ```js\n144: let loopId = null;\n145: function startLoop() {\n146:   if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);\n147:   function tick(now) { /* ... */ loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick); }\n148:   loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n149: }\n150: ```\n151: \n152: ### 7. Game-over overlay shows score and restart instructions\n153: \n154: When the player dies, draw on the canvas: \"Game Over\", final score, current high score (if you persist one), and \"Press R or click Restart\". Do not rely on the HTML restart button alone — players need to know they can restart.\n155: \n156: ## Observable State Contract — required for playtest\n157: \n158: In addition to the rules above, your game **must** expose its current state on `window.__game` so the automated playtest can observe what the player would observe. A game that does not expose this object is rejected.\n159: \n160: ```js\n161: window.__game = {\n162:   state: \"menu\",        // \"menu\" | \"playing\" | \"gameover\" — exact strings, no others\n163:   score: 0,             // current score, number\n164:   player: {\n165:     x: 400,             // player x in canvas coordinates (0 = left)\n166:     y: 300,             // player y in canvas coordinates (0 = top)\n167:     visible: true,      // true if the player is currently rendered inside the canvas viewport\n168:   },\n169:   objective: \"Dodge falling neon blocks. Survive as long as possible.\", // 10-200 chars, one sentence\n170: };\n171: ```\n172: \n173: Rules:\n174: - The object must exist from the very first frame, before any input.\n175: - Update `state`, `score`, `player.x`, `player.y`, and `player.visible` whenever they change. The simplest approach is to update them at the end of your render/update step each frame.\n176: - `state` must only ever be one of the three exact strings. No other values.\n177: - `player.visible` is critical. **If your camera follows the player, the camera math must keep `player.visible` = true at all times.** A camera that lags the player off-screen is the most common failure here.\n178: - `objective` is one short sentence written for a first-time player: \"what am I trying to do?\" — derivable from observing the game alone, not from reading the meta.\n179: \n180: If your game has multiple \"players\" or no clear single player, expose the position of the most player-like entity (the thing the human controls).\n181: \n182: ## What the validator checks\n183: \n184: Your output is checked programmatically. Read CONVENTIONS.md and DESIGN.md in your working directory before doing anything else.

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Momentum Flip
Concept: The dot constantly accelerates forward; each tap instantly reverses its velocity vector, letting you dodge incoming wall spikes that only appear in the current direction of travel.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called your write/edit tool with `TASKS.md` as the path.
